Removent open-source acknowledgements

Alkinum distributes this app with open-source software. The following is a conservative resolved dependency inventory; it includes build-time dependencies and alternatives not all present in a final binary.

Wycheproof Test Vectors

developers to test against known attacks, specification inconsistencies, and other various implementation bugs.
